CREATE TABLE `andares` (
  `id_andar` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_estabelecimento` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `nome_andar` varchar(25) NOT NULL,
  `grid_x` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
  `grid_y` int(11) NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
  `inativo` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `data_cadastro` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `data_ultima_modificacao` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `data_inativacao` datetime DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

CREATE TABLE `elementos_mapa` (
  `id_elemento` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_andar` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_setor` int(11) DEFAULT NULL,
  `tipo` enum('ESTRADA','ENTRADA','SAIDA','ACESSO','FUNDO_SETOR') NOT NULL,
  `orientacao` enum('H','V','NENHUMA') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NENHUMA',
  `coord_x` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `coord_y` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `data_cadastro` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp()
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

CREATE TABLE `equipamentos` (
  `id_equipamento` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `id_setor` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `codigo_mac` varchar(17) NOT NULL,
  `inativo` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `data_cadastro` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `data_ultima_modificacao` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `data_inativacao` datetime DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

CREATE TABLE `estabelecimentos` (
  `id_estabelecimento` int(11) NOT NULL,

  `inativo` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `data_cadastro` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `data_ultima_modificacao` datetime DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `data_inativacao` datetime DEFAULT NULL
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

CREATE TABLE `historico_ocupacao` (

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

  ADD CONSTRAINT `usuario_estabelecimento_ibfk_2` FOREIGN KEY (`id_estabelecimento`) REFERENCES `estabelecimentos` (`id_estabelecimento`) ON DELETE CASCADE;

ALTER TABLE `vagas`

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message taken by
# applypatch from an e-mail message.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an
# appropriate message if it wants to stop the commit.  The hook is
# allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "applypatch-msg".

. git-sh-setup
commitmsg="$(git rev-parse --git-path hooks/commit-msg)"
test -x "$commitmsg" && exec "$commitmsg" ${1+"$@"}
:


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\applypatch-msg.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\commit-msg.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with one argument, the name of the file
# that has the commit message.  The hook should exit with non-zero
# status after issuing an appropriate message if it wants to stop the
# commit.  The hook is allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "commit-msg".

# Uncomment the below to add a Signed-off-by line to the message.
# Doing this in a hook is a bad idea in general, but the prepare-commit-msg
# hook is more suited to it.
#
# SOB=$(git var GIT_AUTHOR_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# grep -qs "^$SOB" "$1" || echo "$SOB" >> "$1"

# This example catches duplicate Signed-off-by lines.

test "" = "$(grep '^Signed-off-by: ' "$1" |
	 sort | uniq -c | sed -e '/^[ 	]*1[ 	]/d')" || {
	echo >&2 Duplicate Signed-off-by lines.
	exit 1
}


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\commit-msg.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\fsmonitor-watchman.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/usr/bin/perl

use strict;
use warnings;
use IPC::Open2;

# An example hook script to integrate Watchman
# (https://facebook.github.io/watchman/) with git to speed up detecting
# new and modified files.
#
# The hook is passed a version (currently 2) and last update token
# formatted as a string and outputs to stdout a new update token and
# all files that have been modified since the update token. Paths must
# be relative to the root of the working tree and separated by a single NUL.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "query-watchman" and set
# 'git config core.fsmonitor .git/hooks/query-watchman'
#
my ($version, $last_update_token) = @ARGV;

# Uncomment for debugging
# print STDERR "$0 $version $last_update_token\n";

# Check the hook interface version
if ($version ne 2) {
	die "Unsupported query-fsmonitor hook version '$version'.\n" .
	    "Falling back to scanning...\n";
}

my $git_work_tree = get_working_dir();

my $retry = 1;

my $json_pkg;
eval {
	require JSON::XS;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::XS";
	1;
} or do {
	require JSON::PP;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::PP";
};

launch_watchman();

sub launch_watchman {
	my $o = watchman_query();
	if (is_work_tree_watched($o)) {
		output_result($o->{clock}, @{$o->{files}});
	}
}

sub output_result {
	my ($clockid, @files) = @_;

	# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
	# binmode $fh, ":utf8";
	# print $fh "$clockid\n@files\n";
	# close $fh;

	binmode STDOUT, ":utf8";
	print $clockid;
	print "\0";
	local $, = "\0";
	print @files;
}

sub watchman_clock {
	my $response = qx/watchman clock "$git_work_tree"/;
	die "Failed to get clock id on '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub watchman_query {
	my $pid = open2(\*CHLD_OUT, \*CHLD_IN, 'watchman -j --no-pretty')
	or die "open2() failed: $!\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n";

	# In the query expression below we're asking for names of files that
	# changed since $last_update_token but not from the .git folder.
	#
	# To accomplish this, we're using the "since" generator to use the
	# recency index to select candidate nodes and "fields" to limit the
	# output to file names only. Then we're using the "expression" term to
	# further constrain the results.
	my $last_update_line = "";
	if (substr($last_update_token, 0, 1) eq "c") {
		$last_update_token = "\"$last_update_token\"";
		$last_update_line = qq[\n"since": $last_update_token,];
	}
	my $query = <<"	END";
		["query", "$git_work_tree", {$last_update_line
			"fields": ["name"],
			"expression": ["not", ["dirname", ".git"]]
		}]
	END

	# Uncomment for debugging the watchman query
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-query.json");
	# print $fh $query;
	# close $fh;

	print CHLD_IN $query;
	close CHLD_IN;
	my $response = do {local $/; <CHLD_OUT>};

	# Uncomment for debugging the watch response
	# open ($fh, ">", ".git/watchman-response.json");
	# print $fh $response;
	# close $fh;

	die "Watchman: command returned no output.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $response eq "";
	die "Watchman: command returned invalid output: $response\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" unless $response =~ /^\{/;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub is_work_tree_watched {
	my ($output) = @_;
	my $error = $output->{error};
	if ($retry > 0 and $error and $error =~ m/unable to resolve root .* directory (.*) is not watched/) {
		$retry--;
		my $response = qx/watchman watch "$git_work_tree"/;
		die "Failed to make watchman watch '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		    "Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;
		$output = $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
		$error = $output->{error};
		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
		# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
		# close $fh;

		# Watchman will always return all files on the first query so
		# return the fast "everything is dirty" flag to git and do the
		# Watchman query just to get it over with now so we won't pay
		# the cost in git to look up each individual file.
		my $o = watchman_clock();
		$error = $output->{error};

		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		output_result($o->{clock}, ("/"));
		$last_update_token = $o->{clock};

		eval { launch_watchman() };
		return 0;
	}

	die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

	return 1;
}

sub get_working_dir {
	my $working_dir;
	if ($^O =~ 'msys' || $^O =~ 'cygwin') {
		$working_dir = Win32::GetCwd();
		$working_dir =~ tr/\\/\//;
	} else {
		require Cwd;
		$working_dir = Cwd::cwd();
	}

	return $working_dir;
}

// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-commit.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git commit" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message if
# it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-commit".

if git rev-parse --verify HEAD >/dev/null 2>&1
then
	against=HEAD
else
	# Initial commit: diff against an empty tree object
	against=$(git hash-object -t tree /dev/null)
fi

# If you want to allow non-ASCII filenames set this variable to true.
allownonascii=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allownonascii)

# Redirect output to stderr.
exec 1>&2

# Cross platform projects tend to avoid non-ASCII filenames; prevent
# them from being added to the repository. We exploit the fact that the
# printable range starts at the space character and ends with tilde.
if [ "$allownonascii" != "true" ] &&
	# Note that the use of brackets around a tr range is ok here, (it's
	# even required, for portability to Solaris 10's /usr/bin/tr), since
	# the square bracket bytes happen to fall in the designated range.
	test $(git diff-index --cached --name-only --diff-filter=A -z $against |
	  LC_ALL=C tr -d '[ -~]\0' | wc -c) != 0
then
	cat <<\EOF
Error: Attempt to add a non-ASCII file name.

This can cause problems if you want to work with people on other platforms.

To be portable it is advisable to rename the file.

If you know what you are doing you can disable this check using:

  git config hooks.allownonascii true
EOF
	exit 1
fi

# If there are whitespace errors, print the offending file names and fail.
exec git diff-index --check --cached $against --


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-commit.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-push.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to verify what is about to be pushed.  Called by "git
# push" after it has checked the remote status, but before anything has been
# pushed.  If this script exits with a non-zero status nothing will be pushed.
#
# This hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- Name of the remote to which the push is being done
# $2 -- URL to which the push is being done
#
# If pushing without using a named remote those arguments will be equal.
#
# Information about the commits which are being pushed is supplied as lines to
# the standard input in the form:
#
#   <local ref> <local oid> <remote ref> <remote oid>
#
# This sample shows how to prevent push of commits where the log message starts
# with "WIP" (work in progress).

remote="$1"
url="$2"

zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')

while read local_ref local_oid remote_ref remote_oid
do
	if test "$local_oid" = "$zero"
	then
		# Handle delete
		:
	else
		if test "$remote_oid" = "$zero"
		then
			# New branch, examine all commits
			range="$local_oid"
		else
			# Update to existing branch, examine new commits
			range="$remote_oid..$local_oid"
		fi

		# Check for WIP commit
		commit=$(git rev-list -n 1 --grep '^WIP' "$range")
		if test -n "$commit"
		then
			echo >&2 "Found WIP commit in $local_ref, not pushing"
			exit 1
		fi
	fi
done

exit 0


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-push.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-rebase.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh
#
# Copyright (c) 2006, 2008 Junio C Hamano
#
# The "pre-rebase" hook is run just before "git rebase" starts doing
# its job, and can prevent the command from running by exiting with
# non-zero status.
#
# The hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- the upstream the series was forked from.
# $2 -- the branch being rebased (or empty when rebasing the current branch).
#
# This sample shows how to prevent topic branches that are already
# merged to 'next' branch from getting rebased, because allowing it
# would result in rebasing already published history.

publish=next
basebranch="$1"
if test "$#" = 2
then
	topic="refs/heads/$2"
else
	topic=`git symbolic-ref HEAD` ||
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt rebasing detached HEAD
fi

case "$topic" in
refs/heads/??/*)
	;;
*)
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt others.
	;;
esac

# Now we are dealing with a topic branch being rebased
# on top of master.  Is it OK to rebase it?

# Does the topic really exist?
git show-ref -q "$topic" || {
	echo >&2 "No such branch $topic"
	exit 1
}

# Is topic fully merged to master?
not_in_master=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^master "$topic"`
if test -z "$not_in_master"
then
	echo >&2 "$topic is fully merged to master; better remove it."
	exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
fi

# Is topic ever merged to next?  If so you should not be rebasing it.
only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort`
only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master           ${publish} | sort`
if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2"
then
	not_in_topic=`git rev-list "^$topic" master`
	if test -z "$not_in_topic"
	then
		echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master"
		exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
	else
		exit 0
	fi
else
	not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"`
	/usr/bin/perl -e '
		my $topic = $ARGV[0];
		my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n";
		my (%not_in_next) = map {
			/^([0-9a-f]+) /;
			($1 => 1);
		} split(/\n/, $ARGV[1]);
		for my $elem (map {
				/^([0-9a-f]+) (.*)$/;
				[$1 => $2];
			} split(/\n/, $ARGV[2])) {
			if (!exists $not_in_next{$elem->[0]}) {
				if ($msg) {
					print STDERR $msg;
					undef $msg;
				}
				print STDERR " $elem->[1]\n";
			}
		}
	' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master"
	exit 1
fi

<<\DOC_END

This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been
published from being rewound.

The workflow assumed here is:

 * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never
   merged into it again (either directly or indirectly).

 * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master",
   it is deleted.  If you need to build on top of it to correct
   earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at
   the tip of the "master".  This is not strictly necessary, but
   it makes it easier to keep your history simple.

 * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic
   branches, merge them into "next" branch.

The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name
to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via
$GIT_DIR/config mechanism.

With this workflow, you would want to know:

(1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next".  Young
    topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather
    clean up before publishing, and things that have not been
    merged into other branches can be easily rebased without
    affecting other people.  But once it is published, you would
    not want to rewind it.

(2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master".
    Then you can delete it.  More importantly, you should not
    build on top of it -- other people may already want to
    change things related to the topic as patches against your
    "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to
    fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the
    tip of "master".

Let's look at this example:

		   o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next"
		  /       /           /           /
		 /   a---a---b A     /           /
		/   /               /           /
	       /   /   c---c---c---c B         /
	      /   /   /             \         /
	     /   /   /   b---b C     \       /
	    /   /   /   /             \     /
    ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master"


A, B and C are topic branches.

 * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next".

 * B has finished.  It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next",
   and is ready to be deleted.

 * C has not merged to "next" at all.

We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage
B to be deleted.

To compute (1):

	git rev-list ^master ^topic next
	git rev-list ^master        next

	if these match, topic has not merged in next at all.

To compute (2):

	git rev-list master..topic

	if this is empty, it is fully merged to "master".

DOC_END


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-rebase.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-receive.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to make use of push options.
# The example simply echoes all push options that start with 'echoback='
# and rejects all pushes when the "reject" push option is used.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-receive".

if test -n "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
then
	i=0
	while test "$i" -lt "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
	do
		eval "value=\$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_$i"
		case "$value" in
		echoback=*)
			echo "echo from the pre-receive-hook: ${value#*=}" >&2
			;;
		reject)
			exit 1
		esac
		i=$((i + 1))
	done
fi


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\pre-receive.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to prepare the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with the name of the file that has the

# "# Please enter the commit message..." help message.

# into the message, just before the "git status" output.  It is
# commented because it doesn't cope with --amend or with squashed

# still be edited.  This is rarely a good idea.

COMMIT_MSG_FILE=$1
COMMIT_SOURCE=$2
SHA1=$3

/usr/bin/perl -i.bak -ne 'print unless(m/^. Please enter the commit message/..m/^#$/)' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"

#       print "\n" . `git diff --cached --name-status -r`

#  *) ;;
# esac

# SOB=$(git var GIT_COMMITTER_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# git interpret-trailers --in-place --trailer "$SOB" "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"
# if test -z "$COMMIT_SOURCE"
# then
#   /usr/bin/perl -i.bak -pe 'print "\n" if !$first_line++' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"
# fi

// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\push-to-checkout.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to update a checked-out tree on a git push.
#
# This hook is invoked by git-receive-pack(1) when it reacts to git
# push and updates reference(s) in its repository, and when the push
# tries to update the branch that is currently checked out and the
# receive.denyCurrentBranch configuration variable is set to
# updateInstead.
#
# By default, such a push is refused if the working tree and the index
# of the remote repository has any difference from the currently
# checked out commit; when both the working tree and the index match
# the current commit, they are updated to match the newly pushed tip
# of the branch. This hook is to be used to override the default
# behaviour; however the code below reimplements the default behaviour
# as a starting point for convenient modification.
#
# The hook receives the commit with which the tip of the current
# branch is going to be updated:
commit=$1

# It can exit with a non-zero status to refuse the push (when it does
# so, it must not modify the index or the working tree).
die () {
	echo >&2 "$*"
	exit 1
}

# Or it can make any necessary changes to the working tree and to the
# index to bring them to the desired state when the tip of the current
# branch is updated to the new commit, and exit with a zero status.
#
# For example, the hook can simply run git read-tree -u -m HEAD "$1"
# in order to emulate git fetch that is run in the reverse direction
# with git push, as the two-tree form of git read-tree -u -m is
# essentially the same as git switch or git checkout that switches
# branches while keeping the local changes in the working tree that do
# not interfere with the difference between the branches.

# The below is a more-or-less exact translation to shell of the C code
# for the default behaviour for git's push-to-checkout hook defined in
# the push_to_deploy() function in builtin/receive-pack.c.
#
# Note that the hook will be executed from the repository directory,
# not from the working tree, so if you want to perform operations on
# the working tree, you will have to adapt your code accordingly, e.g.
# by adding "cd .." or using relative paths.

if ! git update-index -q --ignore-submodules --refresh
then
	die "Up-to-date check failed"
fi

if ! git diff-files --quiet --ignore-submodules --
then
	die "Working directory has unstaged changes"
fi

# This is a rough translation of:
#
#   head_has_history() ? "HEAD" : EMPTY_TREE_SHA1_HEX
if git cat-file -e HEAD 2>/dev/null
then
	head=HEAD
else
	head=$(git hash-object -t tree --stdin </dev/null)
fi

if ! git diff-index --quiet --cached --ignore-submodules $head --
then
	die "Working directory has staged changes"
fi

if ! git read-tree -u -m "$commit"
then
	die "Could not update working tree to new HEAD"
fi


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\push-to-checkout.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\sendemail-validate.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to validate a patch (and/or patch series) before
# sending it via email.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate
# message if it wants to prevent the email(s) from being sent.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "sendemail-validate".
#
# By default, it will only check that the patch(es) can be applied on top of
# the default upstream branch without conflicts in a secondary worktree. After
# validation (successful or not) of the last patch of a series, the worktree
# will be deleted.
#
# The following config variables can be set to change the default remote and
# remote ref that are used to apply the patches against:
#
#   sendemail.validateRemote (default: origin)
#   sendemail.validateRemoteRef (default: HEAD)
#
# Replace the TODO placeholders with appropriate checks according to your
# needs.

validate_cover_letter () {
	file="$1"
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks (e.g. spell checking).
	true
}

validate_patch () {
	file="$1"
	# Ensure that the patch applies without conflicts.
	git am -3 "$file" || return
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for this patch
	# (e.g. checkpatch.pl).
	true
}

validate_series () {
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for the whole series
	# (e.g. quick build, coding style checks, etc.).
	true
}

# main -------------------------------------------------------------------------

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = 1
then
	remote=$(git config --default origin --get sendemail.validateRemote) &&
	ref=$(git config --default HEAD --get sendemail.validateRemoteRef) &&
	worktree=$(mktemp --tmpdir -d sendemail-validate.XXXXXXX) &&
	git worktree add -fd --checkout "$worktree" "refs/remotes/$remote/$ref" &&
	git config --replace-all sendemail.validateWorktree "$worktree"
else
	worktree=$(git config --get sendemail.validateWorktree)
fi || {
	echo "sendemail-validate: error: failed to prepare worktree" >&2
	exit 1
}

unset GIT_DIR GIT_WORK_TREE
cd "$worktree" &&

if grep -q "^diff --git " "$1"
then
	validate_patch "$1"
else
	validate_cover_letter "$1"
fi &&

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_TOTAL"
then
	git config --unset-all sendemail.validateWorktree &&
	trap 'git worktree remove -ff "$worktree"' EXIT &&
	validate_series
fi


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\sendemail-validate.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\update.sample
// ==================================================================

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to block unannotated tags from entering.
# Called by "git receive-pack" with arguments: refname sha1-old sha1-new
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "update".
#
# Config
# ------
# hooks.allowunannotated
#   This boolean sets whether unannotated tags will be allowed into the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletetag
#   This boolean sets whether deleting tags will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowmodifytag
#   This boolean sets whether a tag may be modified after creation. By default
#   it won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletebranch
#   This boolean sets whether deleting branches will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.denycreatebranch
#   This boolean sets whether remotely creating branches will be denied
#   in the repository.  By default this is allowed.
#

# --- Command line
refname="$1"
oldrev="$2"
newrev="$3"

# --- Safety check
if [ -z "$GIT_DIR" ]; then
	echo "Don't run this script from the command line." >&2
	echo " (if you want, you could supply GIT_DIR then run" >&2
	echo "  $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>)" >&2
	exit 1
fi

if [ -z "$refname" -o -z "$oldrev" -o -z "$newrev" ]; then
	echo "usage: $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>" >&2
	exit 1
fi

# --- Config
allowunannotated=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowunannotated)
allowdeletebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletebranch)
denycreatebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.denycreatebranch)
allowdeletetag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletetag)
allowmodifytag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowmodifytag)

# check for no description
projectdesc=$(sed -e '1q' "$GIT_DIR/description")
case "$projectdesc" in
"Unnamed repository"* | "")
	echo "*** Project description file hasn't been set" >&2
	exit 1
	;;
esac

# --- Check types
# if $newrev is 0000...0000, it's a commit to delete a ref.
zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')
if [ "$newrev" = "$zero" ]; then
	newrev_type=delete
else
	newrev_type=$(git cat-file -t $newrev)
fi

case "$refname","$newrev_type" in
	refs/tags/*,commit)
		# un-annotated tag
		short_refname=${refname##refs/tags/}
		if [ "$allowunannotated" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** The un-annotated tag, $short_refname, is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			echo "*** Use 'git tag [ -a | -s ]' for tags you want to propagate." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,delete)
		# delete tag
		if [ "$allowdeletetag" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tag is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,tag)
		# annotated tag
		if [ "$allowmodifytag" != "true" ] && git rev-parse $refname > /dev/null 2>&1
		then
			echo "*** Tag '$refname' already exists." >&2
			echo "*** Modifying a tag is not allowed in this repository." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,commit)
		# branch
		if [ "$oldrev" = "$zero" -a "$denycreatebranch" = "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Creating a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,delete)
		# delete branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,commit)
		# tracking branch
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,delete)
		# delete tracking branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tracking branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	*)
		# Anything else (is there anything else?)
		echo "*** Update hook: unknown type of update to ref $refname of type $newrev_type" >&2
		exit 1
		;;
esac

# --- Finished
exit 0


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: .git\hooks\update.sample
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: .git\info\exclude
// ==================================================================

# git ls-files --others --exclude-from=.git/info/exclude

import aiomysql


class Database:
    def __init__(self):
        self.pool: aiomysql.Pool | None = None

    async def connect(self):
        if self.pool is not None:
            return
        self.pool = await aiomysql.create_pool(
            minsize=1,
            maxsize=10,
            host="127.0.0.1",
            port=3306,
            user="root",
            password="",
            db="vagacerta_db",
            autocommit=True,
            cursorclass=aiomysql.DictCursor,
        )
        print("[DB] ✅ Conectado ao MySQL (vagacerta_db) com sucesso.")

    async def close(self):
        if self.pool is not None:
            self.pool.close()
            await self.pool.wait_closed()
            self.pool = None

    async def execute(self, query: str, params: tuple = ()) -> int:
        async with self.pool.acquire() as conn:
            async with conn.cursor() as cursor:
                await cursor.execute(query, params)
                return cursor.lastrowid or cursor.rowcount

    async def fetch(self, query: str, params: tuple = (), one: bool = False):
        async with self.pool.acquire() as conn:
            async with conn.cursor() as cursor:
                await cursor.execute(query, params)
                if one:
                    return await cursor.fetchone()
                return await cursor.fetchall()


db = Database()


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: backend\database.py
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\main.py
// ==================================================================

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from database import db
from routes import auth, estabelecimentos, vagas


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    await db.connect()
    try:
        await db.execute(
            """
            ALTER TABLE usuarios
            MODIFY nivel_acesso ENUM('admin_dev','admin_estabelecimento','usuario')
            NOT NULL DEFAULT 'usuario'
            """
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
    yield
    await db.close()


app = FastAPI(title="API Vaga Certa", lifespan=lifespan)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

app.include_router(auth.router, tags=["Autenticação e Usuários"])
app.include_router(estabelecimentos.router, tags=["Estabelecimentos e Mapas 2D"])
app.include_router(vagas.router, tags=["Operação de Vagas"])


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: backend\main.py
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\schemas.py
// ==================================================================

from typing import List, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel


class SetorPayload(BaseModel):
    id_temp: int
    nome_setor: str
    prefixo: str
    cor_hex: str


class CelulaPreenchidaPayload(BaseModel):
    x: int
    y: int
    tipo: str
    orientacao: str = "NENHUMA"
    setor_id_temp: Optional[int] = None
    identificador: Optional[str] = None
    livre: Optional[bool] = True
    mac_esp: Optional[str] = None


class AndarPayload(BaseModel):
    nome_andar: str
    grid_x: int
    grid_y: int
    setores: List[SetorPayload]
    celulas_preenchidas: List[CelulaPreenchidaPayload]


class EstabelecimentoSavePayload(BaseModel):
    nome: str
    cidade: str = "Sorocaba"
    id_usuario: int
    andares: List[AndarPayload]


class CadastroUsuarioPayload(BaseModel):
    nome: str
    email: str
    senha: str


class LoginUsuarioPayload(BaseModel):
    email: str
    senha: str


class LoteStatusVagasPayload(BaseModel):
    ids_vagas: List[int]
    inativo: bool


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: backend\schemas.py
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\security.py
// ==================================================================

import hashlib
import hmac
import re
import secrets
from fastapi import HTTPException


def gerar_hash_senha(senha: str) -> str:
    salt = secrets.token_hex(16)
    derivado = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
        "sha256", senha.encode("utf-8"), salt.encode("utf-8"), 100_000
    ).hex()
    return f"pbkdf2_sha256${salt}${derivado}"


def verificar_hash_senha(senha_digitada: str, hash_salvo: str) -> bool:
    if not hash_salvo or "$" not in hash_salvo:
        return False
    try:
        algoritmo, salt, derivado_salvo = hash_salvo.split("$", 2)
        if algoritmo != "pbkdf2_sha256":
            return False
        derivado_teste = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
            "sha256", senha_digitada.encode("utf-8"), salt.encode("utf-8"), 100_000
        ).hex()
        return hmac.compare_digest(derivado_teste, derivado_salvo)
    except Exception:
        return False


def validar_regras_senha(senha: str, email: str):
    if len(senha) < 8:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha deve ter ao menos 8 caracteres."
        )
    if " " in senha:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha não pode conter espaços em branco."
        )
    if not re.search(r"[A-Z]", senha):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha deve conter ao menos uma letra maiúscula."
        )
    if not re.search(r"[a-z]", senha):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha deve conter ao menos uma letra minúscula."
        )
    if not re.search(r"\d", senha):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha deve conter ao menos um número."
        )
    if not re.search(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", senha):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="A senha deve conter ao menos um símbolo."
        )

    email_limpo = email.strip().lower()
    usuario_email = email_limpo.split("@")[0] if "@" in email_limpo else email_limpo
    senha_lower = senha.lower()

    if senha_lower == email_limpo or (
        len(usuario_email) >= 3 and usuario_email in senha_lower
    ):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="A senha não pode conter nem ser parecida com o seu endereço de e-mail.",
        )

// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\routes\auth.py

import re
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, status
from database import db
from schemas import CadastroUsuarioPayload, LoginUsuarioPayload
from security import gerar_hash_senha, verificar_hash_senha, validar_regras_senha

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/usuarios")
async def listar_usuarios():
    return await db.fetch(
        "SELECT id_usuario, nome, email, nivel_acesso FROM usuarios WHERE inativo = 0 ORDER BY id_usuario"
    )


@router.post("/auth/cadastro", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def cadastrar_usuario(payload: CadastroUsuarioPayload):
    nome = payload.nome.strip()
    email = payload.email.strip().lower()
    senha = payload.senha

    if len(nome) < 3:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="Informe seu nome completo (mínimo 3 caracteres)."
        )
    if not re.match(r"^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$", email):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400, detail="Informe um endereço de e-mail válido."
        )

    validar_regras_senha(senha, email)

    existente = await db.fetch(
        "SELECT id_usuario FROM usuarios WHERE email = %s",
        (email,),
        one=True,
    )
    if existente:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409, detail="Este e-mail já está cadastrado no sistema."
        )

    senha_hash = gerar_hash_senha(senha)
    novo_id = await db.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO usuarios (nome, email, senha_hash, nivel_acesso)
        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 'usuario')
        """,
        (nome, email, senha_hash),
    )

    return {
        "status": "sucesso",
        "usuario": {
            "id_usuario": novo_id,
            "nome": nome,
            "email": email,
            "nivel_acesso": "usuario",
        },
    }


@router.post("/auth/login")
async def login_usuario(payload: LoginUsuarioPayload):
    email = payload.email.strip().lower()
    usuario = await db.fetch(
        """
        SELECT id_usuario, nome, email, senha_hash, nivel_acesso, inativo
        FROM usuarios
        WHERE email = %s
        """,
        (email,),
        one=True,
    )

    if not usuario or not verificar_hash_senha(payload.senha, usuario["senha_hash"]):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="E-mail ou senha incorretos.")

    if usuario["inativo"]:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=403, detail="Esta conta está inativa. Contate o suporte."
        )

    return {
        "status": "sucesso",
        "usuario": {
            "id_usuario": usuario["id_usuario"],
            "nome": usuario["nome"],
            "email": usuario["email"],
            "nivel_acesso": usuario["nivel_acesso"],
        },
    }


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: backend\routes\auth.py
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\routes\estabelecimentos.py
// ==================================================================

from typing import List
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, status
from database import db
from schemas import AndarPayload, EstabelecimentoSavePayload

router = APIRouter()


async def gravar_andares_e_mapa(
    cursor, id_estabelecimento: int, andares: List[AndarPayload]
):
    for andar in andares:
        await cursor.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO andares (id_estabelecimento, nome_andar, grid_x, grid_y)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
            """,
            (id_estabelecimento, andar.nome_andar.strip(), andar.grid_x, andar.grid_y),
        )
        id_andar_banco = cursor.lastrowid

        mapa_setores_id = {}
        for setor in andar.setores:
            await cursor.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO setores (id_andar, nome_setor, prefixo, cor_hex)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s)
                """,
                (
                    id_andar_banco,
                    setor.nome_setor.strip(),
                    setor.prefixo.strip().upper(),
                    setor.cor_hex,
                ),
            )
            mapa_setores_id[setor.id_temp] = cursor.lastrowid

        mapa_equipamentos_id = {}

        for cel in andar.celulas_preenchidas:
            id_setor_real = (
                mapa_setores_id.get(cel.setor_id_temp) if cel.setor_id_temp else None
            )

            if cel.tipo == "VAGA":
                if not id_setor_real or not cel.mac_esp:
                    raise HTTPException(
                        status_code=400,
                        detail=f"A vaga na coordenada ({cel.x},{cel.y}) está sem setor ou sem MAC de equipamento.",
                    )
                chave_eq = (id_setor_real, cel.mac_esp.strip().upper())
                if chave_eq not in mapa_equipamentos_id:
                    await cursor.execute(
                        """
                        INSERT INTO equipamentos (id_setor, codigo_mac)
                        VALUES (%s, %s)
                        """,
                        (id_setor_real, cel.mac_esp.strip().upper()),
                    )
                    mapa_equipamentos_id[chave_eq] = cursor.lastrowid

                id_equip_real = mapa_equipamentos_id[chave_eq]

                await cursor.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO vagas (id_equipamento, identificador, coord_x, coord_y, livre_para_estacionar)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    """,
                    (
                        id_equip_real,
                        (cel.identificador or "V1").strip().upper(),
                        cel.x,
                        cel.y,
                        1 if cel.livre else 0,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                orientacao = (
                    cel.orientacao
                    if cel.orientacao in ("H", "V", "NENHUMA")
                    else "NENHUMA"
                )
                await cursor.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO elementos_mapa (id_andar, id_setor, tipo, orientacao, coord_x, coord_y)
                    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                    """,
                    (id_andar_banco, id_setor_real, cel.tipo, orientacao, cel.x, cel.y),
                )


@router.get("/estabelecimentos")
async def listar_estabelecimentos_completos():
    estabelecimentos_rows = await db.fetch("""
        SELECT e.id_estabelecimento AS id, e.nome, e.cidade, e.inativo,
               COALESCE(ue.id_usuario, 1) AS id_usuario
        FROM estabelecimentos e
        LEFT JOIN usuario_estabelecimento ue ON e.id_estabelecimento = ue.id_estabelecimento
        ORDER BY e.id_estabelecimento ASC
        """)

    resultado = []
    for est in estabelecimentos_rows:
        id_est = est["id"]
        andares_rows = await db.fetch(
            """
            SELECT id_andar, nome_andar, grid_x, grid_y
            FROM andares
            WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s AND inativo = 0
            ORDER BY id_andar ASC
            """,
            (id_est,),
        )

        andares_formatados = []
        for andar in andares_rows:
            id_andar = andar["id_andar"]

            setores_rows = await db.fetch(
                """
                SELECT id_setor, nome_setor, prefixo, cor_hex, inativo AS inativo_offline
                FROM setores
                WHERE id_andar = %s
                ORDER BY id_setor ASC
                """,
                (id_andar,),
            )

            elementos_rows = await db.fetch(
                """
                SELECT id_elemento, id_setor, tipo, orientacao, coord_x, coord_y
                FROM elementos_mapa
                WHERE id_andar = %s
                """,
                (id_andar,),
            )

            vagas_rows = await db.fetch(
                """
                SELECT v.id_vaga, v.identificador, v.coord_x, v.coord_y,
                       v.livre_para_estacionar, v.inativo,
                       eq.id_equipamento, eq.codigo_mac,
                       s.id_setor, s.nome_setor, s.prefixo, s.cor_hex
                FROM vagas v
                JOIN equipamentos eq ON v.id_equipamento = eq.id_equipamento
                JOIN setores s ON eq.id_setor = s.id_setor
                WHERE s.id_andar = %s
                """,
                (id_andar,),
            )

            andares_formatados.append(
                {
                    "id_andar": id_andar,
                    "nome_andar": andar["nome_andar"],
                    "grid_x": andar["grid_x"],
                    "grid_y": andar["grid_y"],
                    "setores": [
                        {
                            "id_setor": s["id_setor"],
                            "nome_setor": s["nome_setor"],
                            "prefixo": s["prefixo"],
                            "cor_hex": s["cor_hex"],
                            "inativo_offline": bool(s["inativo_offline"]),
                        }
                        for s in setores_rows
                    ],
                    "elementos_mapa": elementos_rows,
                    "vagas": [
                        {
                            "id_vaga": v["id_vaga"],
                            "identificador": v["identificador"],
                            "coord_x": v["coord_x"],
                            "coord_y": v["coord_y"],
                            "livre": bool(v["livre_para_estacionar"]),
                            "inativo": bool(v["inativo"]),
                            "mac_esp": v["codigo_mac"],
                            "id_setor": v["id_setor"],
                            "nome_setor": v["nome_setor"],
                            "prefixo": v["prefixo"],
                            "cor_hex": v["cor_hex"],
                        }
                        for v in vagas_rows
                    ],
                }
            )

        resultado.append(
            {
                "id": id_est,
                "nome": est["nome"],
                "cidade": est.get("cidade") or "Sorocaba",
                "id_usuario": est["id_usuario"],
                "inativo": bool(est["inativo"]),
                "andares": andares_formatados,
            }
        )

    return resultado


@router.post("/estabelecimentos", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
async def criar_estabelecimento(payload: EstabelecimentoSavePayload):
    cidade_limpa = (payload.cidade or "Sorocaba").strip()
    async with db.pool.acquire() as conn:
        async with conn.cursor() as cursor:
            try:
                await conn.begin()
                await cursor.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO estabelecimentos (nome, cidade) VALUES (%s, %s)",
                    (payload.nome.strip(), cidade_limpa),
                )
                id_est = cursor.lastrowid

                await cursor.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO usuario_estabelecimento (id_usuario, id_estabelecimento) VALUES (%s, %s)",
                    (payload.id_usuario, id_est),
                )

                await gravar_andares_e_mapa(cursor, id_est, payload.andares)
                await conn.commit()
                return {"status": "sucesso", "id_estabelecimento": id_est}
            except Exception as e:
                await conn.rollback()
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400, detail=f"Erro ao salvar no banco: {str(e)}"
                )


@router.put("/estabelecimentos/{id_estabelecimento}")
async def atualizar_estabelecimento(
    id_estabelecimento: int, payload: EstabelecimentoSavePayload
):
    cidade_limpa = (payload.cidade or "Sorocaba").strip()
    async with db.pool.acquire() as conn:
        async with conn.cursor() as cursor:
            try:
                await conn.begin()
                await cursor.execute(
                    "UPDATE estabelecimentos SET nome = %s, cidade = %s WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s",
                    (payload.nome.strip(), cidade_limpa, id_estabelecimento),
                )

                await cursor.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM usuario_estabelecimento WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s",
                    (id_estabelecimento,),
                )
                await cursor.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO usuario_estabelecimento (id_usuario, id_estabelecimento) VALUES (%s, %s)",
                    (payload.id_usuario, id_estabelecimento),
                )

                await cursor.execute(
                    "DELETE FROM andares WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s",
                    (id_estabelecimento,),
                )

                await gravar_andares_e_mapa(cursor, id_estabelecimento, payload.andares)
                await conn.commit()
                return {"status": "sucesso", "id_estabelecimento": id_estabelecimento}
            except Exception as e:
                await conn.rollback()
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=400, detail=f"Erro ao atualizar no banco: {str(e)}"
                )


@router.patch("/estabelecimentos/{id_estabelecimento}/status")
async def alternar_inativo_estabelecimento(id_estabelecimento: int):
    est = await db.fetch(
        "SELECT inativo FROM estabelecimentos WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s",
        (id_estabelecimento,),
        one=True,
    )
    if not est:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Estabelecimento não encontrado.")

    novo_status = 0 if est["inativo"] else 1
    await db.execute(
        """
        UPDATE estabelecimentos
        SET inativo = %s,
            data_inativacao = IF(%s = 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), NULL)
        WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s
        """,
        (novo_status, novo_status, id_estabelecimento),
    )
    return {"status": "sucesso", "inativo": bool(novo_status)}


@router.delete("/estabelecimentos/{id_estabelecimento}")
async def deletar_estabelecimento(id_estabelecimento: int):
    linhas = await db.execute(
        "DELETE FROM estabelecimentos WHERE id_estabelecimento = %s",
        (id_estabelecimento,),
    )
    if linhas == 0:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Estabelecimento não encontrado.")
    return {"status": "sucesso"}


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: backend\routes\estabelecimentos.py
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: backend\routes\vagas.py
// ==================================================================

from fastapi import APIRouter
from database import db
from schemas import LoteStatusVagasPayload

router = APIRouter()


@router.patch("/vagas/status-lote")
async def alterar_status_vagas_em_lote(payload: LoteStatusVagasPayload):
    if not payload.ids_vagas:
        return {"status": "sucesso", "afetadas": 0}

    placeholders = ",".join(["%s"] * len(payload.ids_vagas))
    novo_valor = 1 if payload.inativo else 0
    params = tuple([novo_valor, novo_valor] + payload.ids_vagas)

    await db.execute(
        f"""
        UPDATE vagas
        SET inativo = %s,
            data_inativacao = IF(%s = 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), NULL)
        WHERE id_vaga IN ({placeholders})
        """,
        params,
    )
    return {"status": "sucesso", "inativo": payload.inativo}

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import {
  iniciarNovoFormulario,
  usuarioLogado,
  idsSelecionadosGerente,
  simulacao1Ativa
} from '@/stores/estacionamentoStore'
import { encerrarSessaoUsuario } from '@/stores/authStore'
import {
  IconMenu2,
  IconSquareRoundedX,
  IconSquareRoundedPlus,
  IconSettings,
  IconQrcode,
  IconLogin,
  IconUserPlus,
  IconInfoCircle,
  IconLogout,
  IconLayoutBoard,
  IconParking,
  IconChartBar
} from '@/constants/icons'

const menuAberto = ref(false)
const router = useRouter()

function irPara(rota) {
  menuAberto.value = false
  router.push(rota)
}

function irParaHomePorNivel() {
  menuAberto.value = false
  const nivel = usuarioLogado.value?.nivel_acesso
  if (nivel === 'admin_dev') {
    router.push({ name: 'admin-lista' })
  } else if (nivel === 'admin_estabelecimento') {
    router.push({ name: 'gerente' })
  } else {
    router.push({ name: 'motorista-home' })
  }
}

function irParaNovoEstacionamento() {
  iniciarNovoFormulario()
  menuAberto.value = false
  router.push({ name: 'admin-info-gerais' })
}

function sairDaConta() {
  encerrarSessaoUsuario()
  idsSelecionadosGerente.value = []
  simulacao1Ativa.value = false
  menuAberto.value = false
  router.push({ name: 'motorista-home' })
}
</script>

<template>
  <button class="btn-hamburguer" title="Abrir Menu" @click="menuAberto = !menuAberto">
    <IconMenu2 :size="22" />
  </button>

  <button class="btn-logo-vc" title="Ir para a página inicial" @click="irParaHomePorNivel">
    <span class="vc-letra-v">V</span><span class="vc-letra-c">C</span>
  </button>

  <Transition name="fade-backdrop">
    <div v-if="menuAberto" class="menu-backdrop" @click="menuAberto = false"></div>
  </Transition>

  <Transition name="slide-menu">
    <aside v-if="menuAberto" class="menu-lateral">
      <div class="menu-header">
        <div class="item-icone-texto">
          <span class="badge-logo-menu">VC</span>
          <h2>Vaga Certa</h2>
        </div>
        <button class="btn-fechar" @click="menuAberto = false">
          <IconSquareRoundedX :size="22" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <nav class="menu-links">
        <template v-if="usuarioLogado?.nivel_acesso === 'admin_dev'">
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irParaNovoEstacionamento">
            <IconSquareRoundedPlus :size="19" />
            <span>Novo Estacionamento</span>
          </a>
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'admin-lista' })">
            <IconSettings :size="19" />
            <span>Gerenciar Estacionamentos</span>
          </a>
          <div class="menu-divisor"></div>
        </template>

        <template v-if="usuarioLogado?.nivel_acesso === 'admin_estabelecimento'">
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'gerente' })">
            <IconLayoutBoard :size="19" />
            <span>Dashboard Principal</span>
          </a>
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'gerente-estacionamentos' })">
            <IconParking :size="19" />
            <span>Visão dos Estacionamentos</span>
          </a>
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'gerente-relatorios' })">
            <IconChartBar :size="19" />
            <span>Relatórios e Exportação</span>
          </a>
          <div class="menu-divisor"></div>
        </template>

        <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'motorista-home' })">
          <IconQrcode :size="19" />
          <span>Escanear Estacionamento</span>
        </a>

        <template v-if="!usuarioLogado">
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'login' })">
            <IconLogin :size="19" />
            <span>Login</span>
          </a>
          <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'cadastro' })">
            <IconUserPlus :size="19" />
            <span>Cadastro</span>
          </a>
        </template>

        <a href="#" class="item-icone-texto" @click.prevent="irPara({ name: 'sobre' })">
          <IconInfoCircle :size="19" />
          <span>Sobre</span>
        </a>

        <a
          v-if="usuarioLogado"
          href="#"
          class="item-icone-texto link-sair-menu"
          @click.prevent="sairDaConta"
        >
          <IconLogout :size="19" />
          <span>Sair</span>
        </a>
      </nav>
    </aside>
  </Transition>
</template>

// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\composables\useGridPanZoom.js
// ==================================================================

import { ref } from 'vue'

export function useGridPanZoom() {
  const panX = ref(0)
  const panY = ref(0)
  const zoomEscala = ref(1)

  let toqueUnico = null
  let pinchInicial = null

  function distanciaEntreToques(t1, t2) {
    return Math.hypot(t2.clientX - t1.clientX, t2.clientY - t1.clientY)
  }

  function pontoMedioToques(t1, t2) {
    return {
      x: (t1.clientX + t2.clientX) / 2,
      y: (t1.clientY + t2.clientY) / 2
    }
  }

  function iniciarToquePanZoom(e) {
    if (e.touches.length === 2) {
      toqueUnico = null
      const dist = distanciaEntreToques(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      const meio = pontoMedioToques(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      pinchInicial = {
        dist,
        escala: zoomEscala.value,
        panX: panX.value,
        panY: panY.value,
        meioX: meio.x,
        meioY: meio.y
      }
    } else if (e.touches.length === 1) {
      pinchInicial = null
      const t = e.touches[0]
      toqueUnico = {
        startX: t.clientX,
        startY: t.clientY,
        lastX: t.clientX,
        lastY: t.clientY,
        panXInicial: panX.value,
        panYInicial: panY.value,
        moveu: false
      }
    }
  }

  function moverToquePanZoom(e) {
    if (e.touches.length === 2 && pinchInicial) {
      const novaDist = distanciaEntreToques(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      const novoMeio = pontoMedioToques(e.touches[0], e.touches[1])
      const fator = novaDist / pinchInicial.dist
      zoomEscala.value = Math.min(2.5, Math.max(0.45, pinchInicial.escala * fator))
      panX.value = pinchInicial.panX + (novoMeio.x - pinchInicial.meioX)
      panY.value = pinchInicial.panY + (novoMeio.y - pinchInicial.meioY)
    } else if (e.touches.length === 1 && toqueUnico) {
      const t = e.touches[0]
      toqueUnico.lastX = t.clientX
      toqueUnico.lastY = t.clientY
      const dx = t.clientX - toqueUnico.startX
      const dy = t.clientY - toqueUnico.startY

      if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 7) {
        toqueUnico.moveu = true
        panX.value = toqueUnico.panXInicial + dx
        panY.value = toqueUnico.panYInicial + dy
      }
    }
  }

  function finalizarToquePanZoom(e, aoTocarCelulaSemArrastar) {
    if (e.touches.length < 2) pinchInicial = null
    if (e.touches.length === 0 && toqueUnico) {
      if (!toqueUnico.moveu && typeof aoTocarCelulaSemArrastar === 'function') {
        const el = document.elementFromPoint(toqueUnico.lastX, toqueUnico.lastY)
        const celulaEl = el?.closest('[data-coord]')
        if (celulaEl) {
          const [cx, cy] = celulaEl.getAttribute('data-coord').split(',').map(Number)
          aoTocarCelulaSemArrastar(cx, cy)
        }
      }
      toqueUnico = null
    }
  }

  function resetarCameraGrid() {
    panX.value = 0
    panY.value = 0
    zoomEscala.value = 1
  }

  return {
    panX,
    panY,
    zoomEscala,
    iniciarToquePanZoom,
    moverToquePanZoom,
    finalizarToquePanZoom,
    resetarCameraGrid
  }
}

import { usuarioLogado } from '@/stores/authStore'

export function configurarGuardsRouter(router) {
  router.beforeEach((to, from, next) => {
    if (to.path === '/' && to.query.id) {
      return next({ name: 'motorista-mapa', params: { id: to.query.id } })
    }

    if (to.meta.rolesPermitidas) {
      const usuario = usuarioLogado.value
      if (!usuario || !to.meta.rolesPermitidas.includes(usuario.nivel_acesso)) {
        return next({ name: 'acesso-negado' })
      }
    }

    next()
  })
}

export const API_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:8000'

export async function apiRequest(endpoint, options = {}) {
  const config = {
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      ...(options.headers || {})
    },
    ...options
  }

  const resposta = await fetch(`${API_URL}${endpoint}`, config)
  const dados = await resposta.json().catch(() => ({}))

  if (!resposta.ok) {
    throw new Error(dados.detail || 'Erro na comunicação com o servidor.')
  }

  return dados
}

import { apiRequest } from './api'

export function loginUsuarioApi(email, senha) {
  return apiRequest('/auth/login', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({ email, senha })
  })
}

export function cadastrarUsuarioApi(nome, email, senha) {
  return apiRequest('/auth/cadastro', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: JSON.stringify({ nome, email, senha })
  })
}

export function listarUsuariosApi() {
  return apiRequest('/usuarios')
}

import { apiRequest } from './api'

export function listarEstabelecimentosApi() {
  return apiRequest('/estabelecimentos')
}

export function salvarEstabelecimentoApi(payload, idEdicao = null) {
  const endpoint = idEdicao ? `/estabelecimentos/${idEdicao}` : '/estabelecimentos'
  const method = idEdicao ? 'PUT' : 'POST'
  return apiRequest(endpoint, {
    method,
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  })
}

export function alternarStatusEstabelecimentoApi(id) {
  return apiRequest(`/estabelecimentos/${id}/status`, {
    method: 'PATCH'
  })
}

export function removerEstabelecimentoApi(id) {
  return apiRequest(`/estabelecimentos/${id}`, {
    method: 'DELETE'
  })
}

export function alterarStatusVagasLoteApi(idsVagas, inativo) {
  return apiRequest('/vagas/status-lote', {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: JSON.stringify({ ids_vagas: idsVagas, inativo })
  })
}

import { ref } from 'vue'

export const usuarioLogado = ref(
  JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vagacerta_usuario') || 'null')
)

export function salvarSessaoUsuario(dadosUsuario) {
  usuarioLogado.value = dadosUsuario
  localStorage.setItem('vagacerta_usuario', JSON.stringify(dadosUsuario))
}

export function encerrarSessaoUsuario() {
  usuarioLogado.value = null
  localStorage.removeItem('vagacerta_usuario')
}

import { ref } from 'vue'
import { paletaSetores } from '@/constants/paletaSetores'
import { escurecerCorHex } from '@/utils/colorUtils'
import {
  criarMatrizVazia,
  hidratarEstabelecimentoDoBanco,
  obterAndarEntradaSaida,
  contarVagasEstabelecimento
} from '@/utils/gridUtils'
import { listarUsuariosApi } from '@/services/authService'
import {
  listarEstabelecimentosApi,
  salvarEstabelecimentoApi,
  alternarStatusEstabelecimentoApi,
  removerEstabelecimentoApi
} from '@/services/estacionamentoService'
import { usuarioLogado } from './authStore'
import {
  idsSelecionadosGerente,
  cidadeSelecionadaGerente,
  cidadesDoGerente,
  estabelecimentosDoGerentePorCidade,
  selecionarCidadeGerente,

  estabelecimentosFiltradosGerente,

export const usuariosCadastrados = ref([])
export const estabelecimentos = ref([])
export const carregandoDados = ref(false)

export const modoEdicaoId = ref(null)
export const formEstabelecimento = ref({
  nome: '',
  cidade: 'Sorocaba',
  id_usuario: 1,
  andares: [{ id_andar: Date.now(), nome_andar: 'Subsolo 1', grid_x: 30, grid_y: 20 }]
})

export async function carregarDadosDoBanco() {
  carregandoDados.value = true
  try {
    const [usuarios, listaCrua] = await Promise.all([
      listarUsuariosApi(),
      listarEstabelecimentosApi()
    ])

    usuariosCadastrados.value = usuarios || []
    estabelecimentos.value = (listaCrua || []).map(hidratarEstabelecimentoDoBanco)

    if (simulacao1Ativa.value) {
      simulacao1Ativa.value = false
      alternarSimulacao1Frontend()
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Erro ao conectar com a API FastAPI:', err)
  } finally {
    carregandoDados.value = false
  }
}

export async function salvarEstacionamentoNoBanco() {
  const payload = {
    nome: formEstabelecimento.value.nome,
    cidade: (formEstabelecimento.value.cidade || 'Sorocaba').trim(),
    id_usuario: formEstabelecimento.value.id_usuario,
    andares: formEstabelecimento.value.andares.map((andar) => {
      const celulasPreenchidas = []

      Object.values(andar.celulas || {}).forEach((c) => {
        if (c.tipo === 'VAZIO' && !c.setorId) return
        celulasPreenchidas.push({
          x: c.x,
          y: c.y,
          tipo: c.tipo === 'VAZIO' && c.setorId ? 'FUNDO_SETOR' : c.tipo,
          orientacao: c.orientacao || 'NENHUMA',
          setor_id_temp: c.setorId || null,
          identificador: c.identificador || null,
          livre: c.livre !== false,
          mac_esp: c.macEsp || null
        })
      })

      return {
        nome_andar: andar.nome_andar,
        grid_x: andar.grid_x,
        grid_y: andar.grid_y,
        setores: (andar.setores || []).map(s => ({
          id_temp: s.id_setor,
          nome_setor: s.nome_setor,
          prefixo: s.prefixo,
          cor_hex: s.cor_hex
        })),
        celulas_preenchidas: celulasPreenchidas
      }
    })
  }

  await salvarEstabelecimentoApi(payload, modoEdicaoId.value)
  await carregarDadosDoBanco()
}

export async function alternarInativoNoBanco(est) {
  await alternarStatusEstabelecimentoApi(est.id)
  await carregarDadosDoBanco()
}

export async function removerEstabelecimentoNoBanco(est) {
  await removerEstabelecimentoApi(est.id)
  await carregarDadosDoBanco()
}

export function iniciarNovoFormulario() {
  modoEdicaoId.value = null
  const idPadrao = usuariosCadastrados.value[0]?.id_usuario || 1
  formEstabelecimento.value = {
    nome: '',
    cidade: 'Sorocaba',
    id_usuario: idPadrao,
    andares: [{ id_andar: Date.now(), nome_andar: 'Subsolo 1', grid_x: 30, grid_y: 20 }]
  }
}

export function carregarFormularioEdicao(est) {
  modoEdicaoId.value = est.id
  const copia = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(est))
  copia.cidade = copia.cidade || 'Sorocaba'
  formEstabelecimento.value = copia
}

import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { usuarioLogado } from './authStore'
import { estabelecimentos } from './estacionamentoStore'

export const idsSelecionadosGerente = ref([])
export const cidadeSelecionadaGerente = ref('TODAS')
export const simulacao1Ativa = ref(false)

export const estabelecimentosDoGerente = computed(() => {
  if (!usuarioLogado.value) return []
  return estabelecimentos.value.filter(
    e => !e.inativo && Number(e.id_usuario) === Number(usuarioLogado.value.id_usuario)
  )
})

export const cidadesDoGerente = computed(() => {
  const setCidades = new Set(
    estabelecimentosDoGerente.value
      .map(e => (e.cidade || 'Sorocaba').trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
  )
  return Array.from(setCidades).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, 'pt-BR'))
})

export const estabelecimentosDoGerentePorCidade = computed(() => {
  const todos = estabelecimentosDoGerente.value
  if (!cidadeSelecionadaGerente.value || cidadeSelecionadaGerente.value === 'TODAS') {
    return todos
  }
  return todos.filter(
    e => (e.cidade || 'Sorocaba').toLowerCase() === cidadeSelecionadaGerente.value.toLowerCase()
  )
})

export const estabelecimentosFiltradosGerente = computed(() => {
  const baseCidade = estabelecimentosDoGerentePorCidade.value
  if (idsSelecionadosGerente.value.length === 0) return baseCidade
  const filtrados = baseCidade.filter(e => idsSelecionadosGerente.value.includes(e.id))
  return filtrados.length > 0 ? filtrados : baseCidade
})

export function selecionarCidadeGerente(novaCidade) {
  cidadeSelecionadaGerente.value = novaCidade
  idsSelecionadosGerente.value = []
}

export function alternarSimulacao1Frontend() {
  simulacao1Ativa.value = !simulacao1Ativa.value

  estabelecimentosDoGerente.value.forEach((est) => {
    ;(est.andares || []).forEach((andar) => {
      const vagas = Object.values(andar.celulas || {}).filter(
        c => c.tipo === 'VAGA' && !c.inativa
      )

      if (!simulacao1Ativa.value) {
        vagas.forEach((v) => {
          v.livre = true
          v.usosNoDia = 0
          v.horaChegada = null
          v.duracaoMinutos = 0
        })
        return
      }

      const indices = vagas.map((_, i) => i).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5)
      const limiteOcupadas = Math.round(vagas.length * 0.6)
      const setOcupadas = new Set(indices.slice(0, limiteOcupadas))

      const agora = new Date()
      vagas.forEach((v, idx) => {
        v.usosNoDia = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) + 1
        if (setOcupadas.has(idx)) {
          v.livre = false
          const duracao = Math.floor(Math.random() * 195) + 15
          v.duracaoMinutos = duracao
          const chegada = new Date(agora.getTime() - duracao * 60000)
          v.horaChegada = chegada.toTimeString().slice(0, 5)
        } else {
          v.livre = true
          v.duracaoMinutos = 0
          v.horaChegada = null
        }
      })
    })
  })
}

// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\utils\colorUtils.js
// ==================================================================

import { paletaSetores } from '@/constants/paletaSetores'

export function escurecerCorHex(hex) {
  if (!hex) return '#334155'
  const encontrada = paletaSetores.find(p => p.hex.toLowerCase() === hex.toLowerCase())
  if (encontrada) return encontrada.vagaHex

  const limpo = hex.replace('#', '')
  const num = parseInt(limpo, 16)
  const fator = 0.45
  const r = Math.max(0, Math.floor(((num >> 16) & 255) * fator))
  const g = Math.max(0, Math.floor(((num >> 8) & 255) * fator))
  const b = Math.max(0, Math.floor((num & 255) * fator))
  return `rgb(${r}, ${g}, ${b})`
}


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\utils\colorUtils.js
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\utils\exportUtils.js
// ==================================================================

export function formatarMinutos(mins) {
  if (!mins || mins <= 0) return '0min'
  const h = Math.floor(mins / 60)
  const m = mins % 60
  return h > 0 ? `${h}h ${m}min` : `${m}min`
}

export function formatarLinhasParaCsvBrasil(linhas) {
  return linhas
    .map(row => row.map(val => `"${String(val ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(';'))
    .join('\r\n')
}

export function dispararDownloadArquivo(conteudo, nomeArquivo, mimeType = 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;') {
  const blob = new Blob([conteudo], { type: mimeType })
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = nomeArquivo
  document.body.appendChild(link)
  link.click()
  document.body.removeChild(link)
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}


// 🏁 FIM DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\utils\exportUtils.js
// ------------------------------------------------------------------


// ==================================================================
// 🚀 INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: frontend\src\utils\gridUtils.js
// ==================================================================

export function criarMatrizVazia(largura, altura) {
  const celulas = {}
  for (let y = 1; y <= altura; y++) {
    for (let x = 1; x <= largura; x++) {
      celulas[`${x},${y}`] = {
        x,
        y,
        setorId: null,
        corSetor: null,
        nomeSetor: null,
        prefixoSetor: null,
        tipo: 'VAZIO',
        orientacao: 'NENHUMA',
        vagaId: null,
        identificador: '',
        livre: true,
        inativa: false,
        semEsp: false,
        usosNoDia: 0,
        horaChegada: null,
        duracaoMinutos: 0,
        macEsp: null
      }
    }
  }
  return celulas
}

export function hidratarEstabelecimentoDoBanco(estBanco) {
  const andaresHidratados = (estBanco.andares || []).map((andar) => {
    const celulas = criarMatrizVazia(andar.grid_x, andar.grid_y)
    const mapaSetores = {}
    ;(andar.setores || []).forEach((s) => {
      mapaSetores[s.id_setor] = s
    })

    ;(andar.elementos_mapa || []).forEach((el) => {
      const chave = `${el.coord_x},${el.coord_y}`
      const c = celulas[chave]
      if (!c) return

      if (el.id_setor && mapaSetores[el.id_setor]) {
        const s = mapaSetores[el.id_setor]
        c.setorId = s.id_setor
        c.nomeSetor = s.nome_setor
        c.prefixoSetor = s.prefixo
        c.corSetor = s.cor_hex
      }

      if (el.tipo !== 'FUNDO_SETOR') {
        c.tipo = el.tipo
        c.orientacao = el.orientacao || 'NENHUMA'
      }
    })

    ;(andar.vagas || []).forEach((v) => {
      const chave = `${v.coord_x},${v.coord_y}`
      const c = celulas[chave]
      if (!c) return

      c.tipo = 'VAGA'
      c.vagaId = v.id_vaga
      c.identificador = v.identificador
      c.livre = Boolean(v.livre)
      c.inativa = Boolean(v.inativo)
      c.macEsp = v.mac_esp
      c.semEsp = !v.mac_esp
      c.setorId = v.id_setor
      c.nomeSetor = v.nome_setor
      c.prefixoSetor = v.prefixo
      c.corSetor = v.cor_hex
    })

    return {
      id_andar: andar.id_andar,
      nome_andar: andar.nome_andar,
      grid_x: andar.grid_x,
      grid_y: andar.grid_y,
      setores: andar.setores || [],
      equipamentos: [],
      celulas
    }
  })

  return {
    id: estBanco.id,
    nome: estBanco.nome,
    cidade: estBanco.cidade || 'Sorocaba',
    id_usuario: estBanco.id_usuario,
    inativo: Boolean(estBanco.inativo),
    andares: andaresHidratados
  }
}

export function obterAndarEntradaSaida(est) {
  if (!est?.andares || est.andares.length === 0) return null
  const andarAmbos = est.andares.find((a) => {
    const cels = Object.values(a.celulas || {})
    return cels.some(c => c.tipo === 'ENTRADA') && cels.some(c => c.tipo === 'SAIDA')
  })
  if (andarAmbos) return andarAmbos

  const andarEntrada = est.andares.find((a) =>
    Object.values(a.celulas || {}).some(c => c.tipo === 'ENTRADA' || c.tipo === 'SAIDA')
  )
  return andarEntrada || est.andares[0]
}

export function contarVagasEstabelecimento(est) {
  let total = 0
  let ocupadas = 0
  let livres = 0
  let inativas = 0
  let somaDuracao = 0
  let qtdComDuracao = 0
  let somaUsosDia = 0

  ;(est.andares || []).forEach((andar) => {
    Object.values(andar.celulas || {}).forEach((c) => {
      if (c.tipo === 'VAGA') {
        total++
        somaUsosDia += c.usosNoDia || 0
        if (c.inativa) {
          inativas++
        } else if (c.livre) {
          livres++
        } else {
          ocupadas++
          if (c.duracaoMinutos > 0) {
            somaDuracao += c.duracaoMinutos
            qtdComDuracao++
          }
        }
      }
    })
  })

  const ativas = Math.max(1, total - inativas)
  const taxaOcupacao = total > 0 ? Math.round((ocupadas / ativas) * 100) : 0
  const tempoMedioMin = qtdComDuracao > 0 ? Math.round(somaDuracao / qtdComDuracao) : (ocupadas > 0 ? 78 : 0)

  return {
    total,
    ocupadas,
    livres,
    inativas,
    taxaOcupacao,
    tempoMedioMin,
    somaUsosDia
  }
}

<script setup>
import { ref, computed, onMounted, onUnmounted } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

  paletaSetores,

  salvarEstacionamentoNoBanco
} from '@/stores/estacionamentoStore'

  IconParking,
  IconSquareRoundedChevronDown
} from '@/constants/icons'

const router = useRouter()

if (!formEstabelecimento.value.andares || !formEstabelecimento.value.andares[0]?.celulas) {
  router.replace({ name: 'admin-info-gerais' })
}

const andarEditorIndex = ref(0)
const andarEditorAtual = computed(() => formEstabelecimento.value.andares[andarEditorIndex.value])

// Menus flutuantes (Mobile/Tablet <= 1024px)
const menuTopoMobileAberto = ref(false)
const menuAdicionarMobileAberto = ref(false)
const mostrarPainelAvisos = ref(false)
const salvandoNoBanco = ref(false)
const modalSucessoSalvarAberto = ref(false)

function alternarMenuEngrenagem() {
  menuTopoMobileAberto.value = !menuTopoMobileAberto.value
  if (menuTopoMobileAberto.value) {
    menuAdicionarMobileAberto.value = false
    mostrarPainelAvisos.value = false
  }
}

function alternarMenuAdicionar() {
